+   232	  }
+   233	  function _correlationGroupId(canonical) { return 'corr_' + _hash64('corr|' + canonical); }
+   234	
+   235	  // Validate input envelope shape + take a TRUSTED SNAPSHOT of every primitive field (RN-01).
+   236	  // Returns { ok:true, snapshot:{caseAssociation, generationToken, contextVersion} } so the
+   237	  // caller has a single source of truth that cannot be re-read from the original via accessors.
+   238	  function _validateInputEnvelope(input) {
+   239	    if (!RC.isOriginalPlainObject(input)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.INTERNAL_CONTRACT_VIOLATION], { detail: 'input envelope is not a plain object (Proxy / class instance rejected)' });
+   240	    if (RC.hasHiddenOwnKey(input)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.UNKNOWN_OWN_KEY], { detail: 'input envelope carries Symbol-keyed or non-enumerable own property' });
+   241	    if (!_hasOnlyAllowedKeys(input, INPUT_KEYS)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.UNKNOWN_OWN_KEY], { detail: 'input envelope carries forbidden own key' });
+   242	    // caseAssociation — read via descriptor (RN-01: no caller getter), then deep-validate the clone.
+   243	    var caDesc; try { caDesc = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(input, 'caseAssociation'); }
+   244	    catch (e) { return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_ASSOCIATION_MISMATCH], { detail: 'caseAssociation descriptor read threw' }); }
+   245	    if (!caDesc || !('value' in caDesc)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_ASSOCIATION_MISMATCH], { detail: 'caseAssociation must be a data property' });
+   246	    var ca = caDesc.value;
+   247	    if (!RC.isOriginalPlainObject(ca)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_ASSOCIATION_MISMATCH, CODES.PROTOTYPE_POLLUTION_REJECTED], { detail: 'caseAssociation prototype is not Object.prototype or null' });
+   248	    if (RC.hasHiddenOwnKey(ca)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.UNKNOWN_OWN_KEY], { detail: 'caseAssociation carries Symbol-keyed or non-enumerable own property' });
+   249	    if (RC.hasNonPlainNestedObject(ca)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.PROTOTYPE_POLLUTION_REJECTED], { detail: 'caseAssociation contains a nested non-plain object' });
+   250	    // Clone-then-validate: any read after this point uses the clone (TOCTOU-safe).
+   251	    var caClone = RC.toCleanCopy(ca);
+   252	    if (!_isPlain(caClone)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_ASSOCIATION_MISMATCH], { detail: 'caseAssociation clone is not a plain object' });
+   253	    if (!_hasOnlyAllowedKeys(caClone, CASE_ASSOCIATION_KEYS)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.UNKNOWN_OWN_KEY], { detail: 'caseAssociation carries forbidden own key' });
+   254	    for (var i = 0; i < CASE_ASSOCIATION_REQUIRED.length; i++) {
+   255	      if (!_nonEmptyStr(caClone[CASE_ASSOCIATION_REQUIRED[i]])) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_ASSOCIATION_MISMATCH], { detail: 'caseAssociation.' + CASE_ASSOCIATION_REQUIRED[i] + ' missing' });
+   256	    }
+   257	    if ('lapId' in caClone && caClone.lapId !== null && !_nonEmptyStr(caClone.lapId)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_ASSOCIATION_MISMATCH], { detail: 'caseAssociation.lapId must be null or non-empty string' });
+   258	    // rawEvidence — array gate ONLY (per-node body validates contents independently via per-index descriptor).
+   259	    var reDesc; try { reDesc = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(input, 'rawEvidence'); }
+   260	    catch (e) { return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.INTERNAL_CONTRACT_VIOLATION], { detail: 'rawEvidence descriptor read threw' }); }
+   260	    catch (e) { return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.INTERNAL_CONTRACT_VIOLATION], { detail: 'rawEvidence descriptor read threw' }); }
+   261	    if (!reDesc || !('value' in reDesc)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.INTERNAL_CONTRACT_VIOLATION], { detail: 'rawEvidence must be a data property' });
+   262	    var re = reDesc.value;
+   263	    if (!Array.isArray(re)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.INTERNAL_CONTRACT_VIOLATION], { detail: 'rawEvidence must be an array' });
+   264	    try { if (Object.getPrototypeOf(re) !== Array.prototype) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.PROTOTYPE_POLLUTION_REJECTED], { detail: 'rawEvidence is not a plain Array (subclass / mutated prototype rejected)' }); }
+   265	    catch (e) { return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.PROTOTYPE_POLLUTION_REJECTED], { detail: 'rawEvidence prototype access threw' }); }
+   266	    var reLen;
+   267	    try { reLen = re.length; if (typeof reLen !== 'number' || !_isFiniteNum(reLen) || reLen < 0 || Math.floor(reLen) !== reLen) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.INTERNAL_CONTRACT_VIOLATION], { detail: 'rawEvidence.length malformed' }); }
+   268	    catch (e) { return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.PROTOTYPE_POLLUTION_REJECTED], { detail: 'rawEvidence.length read threw' }); }
+   269	    if (reLen > INPUT_RAW_EVIDENCE_CAP) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.GRAPH_CAP_EXCEEDED], { detail: 'rawEvidence array length ' + reLen + ' exceeds cap ' + INPUT_RAW_EVIDENCE_CAP });
+   270	    // optional generationToken — descriptor-safe read
+   271	    var gt = null;
+   272	    try {
+   273	      var gtDesc = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(input, 'generationToken');
+   274	      if (gtDesc) {
+   275	        if (!('value' in gtDesc)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.INTERNAL_CONTRACT_VIOLATION], { detail: 'generationToken must be a data property' });
+   276	        var gtv = gtDesc.value;
+   277	        if (gtv === null) gt = null;
+   278	        else if (typeof gtv !== 'string' || gtv.length === 0) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.INTERNAL_CONTRACT_VIOLATION], { detail: 'generationToken must be null or non-empty string' });
+   279	        else if (gtv.length > 256) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.BYTE_CAP_EXCEEDED], { detail: 'generationToken exceeds 256 chars' });
+   280	        else gt = gtv;
+   281	      }
+   282	    } catch (e) { return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.INTERNAL_CONTRACT_VIOLATION], { detail: 'generationToken descriptor read threw' }); }
+   283	    // optional contextVersion — descriptor-safe read
+   284	    var cv = null;
+   285	    try {
+   286	      var cvDesc = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(input, 'contextVersion');
+   287	      if (cvDesc) {
+   288	        if (!('value' in cvDesc)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.INTERNAL_CONTRACT_VIOLATION], { detail: 'contextVersion must be a data property' });
+   289	        var cvv = cvDesc.value;
+   290	        if (cvv === null) cv = null;
+   291	        else if (typeof cvv !== 'string' || cvv.length === 0) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.INTERNAL_CONTRACT_VIOLATION], { detail: 'contextVersion must be null or non-empty string' });
+   292	        else if (cvv.length > 64) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.BYTE_CAP_EXCEEDED], { detail: 'contextVersion exceeds 64 chars' });
+   293	        else cv = cvv;
+   294	      }
+   295	    } catch (e) { return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.INTERNAL_CONTRACT_VIOLATION], { detail: 'contextVersion descriptor read threw' }); }
+   296	    return {
+   297	      ok: true,
+   298	      snapshot: {
+   299	        caseAssociation: {
+   300	          caseId: caClone.caseId,
+   301	          sessionId: caClone.sessionId,
+   302	          lapId: ('lapId' in caClone) ? caClone.lapId : null,
+   303	        },
+   304	        rawEvidenceRef: re,           // CALLER ARRAY — never deref by [index]; always Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor
+   305	        rawEvidenceLength: reLen,
+   306	        generationToken: gt,
+   307	        contextVersion: cv,
+   308	      },
+   309	    };
+   310	  }
+   311	
+   312	  // RN-06 closure: resolve opts.clock AFTER envelope validation; descriptor-safe; throwing getter
+   313	  // collapses to createdAt = null (clock is best-effort; never blocks the build).
+   314	  function _resolveClock(opts) {
+   315	    if (opts === null || opts === undefined) return null;
+   316	    if (!RC.isOriginalPlainObject(opts)) return null;
+   317	    if (RC.hasHiddenOwnKey(opts)) return null;
+   318	    if (!_hasOnlyAllowedKeys(opts, OPTS_KEYS)) return null;
+   319	    var desc;
+   320	    try { desc = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(opts, 'clock'); }
+   321	    catch (e) { return null; }
+   322	    if (!desc) return null;
+   323	    if (!('value' in desc)) return null;       // accessor descriptor — fail-soft
+   324	    var clock = desc.value;
+   325	    if (typeof clock !== 'function') return null;
+   326	    try {
+   327	      var v = clock();
+   328	      if (v === null) return null;
+   329	      if (!_isIsoTimestamp(v)) return null;
+   330	      return v;
+   331	    } catch (e) { return null; }
+   332	  }
+   333	
+   334	  // DFS cycle detection over the directed CAUSAL edge list. Uses a proto-null colour map (RN-08).
+   335	  function _findCycle(nodeIds, adjacencyCausal) {
+   336	    var WHITE = 0, GREY = 1, BLACK = 2;
+   337	    var color = Object.create(null);
+   338	    for (var i = 0; i < nodeIds.length; i++) color[nodeIds[i]] = WHITE;
+   339	    function visit(u) {
+   340	      color[u] = GREY;
+   341	      var nbrs = adjacencyCausal[u] || [];
+   342	      for (var k = 0; k < nbrs.length; k++) {
+   343	        var v = nbrs[k];
+   344	        if (color[v] === GREY) return v;
+   345	        if (color[v] === WHITE) {
+   346	          var c = visit(v);
+   347	          if (c !== null) return c;
+   348	        }
+   349	      }
+   350	      color[u] = BLACK;
+   351	      return null;
+   352	    }
+   353	    for (var j = 0; j < nodeIds.length; j++) {
+   354	      var n = nodeIds[j];
+   355	      if (color[n] === WHITE) {
+   356	        var hit = visit(n);
+   357	        if (hit !== null) return hit;
+   358	      }
+   359	    }
+   360	    return null;
+   361	  }
+   362	
+   363	  // Kahn's algorithm — deterministic by tie-breaking on sorted nodeId. Assumes no cycle. Uses
+   364	  // proto-null in-degree map (RN-08).
+   365	  function _topologicalOrder(nodeIds, adjacencyCausal) {
+   366	    var inDegree = Object.create(null);
+   367	    var i;
+   368	    for (i = 0; i < nodeIds.length; i++) inDegree[nodeIds[i]] = 0;
+   369	    for (i = 0; i < nodeIds.length; i++) {
+   370	      var nbrs = adjacencyCausal[nodeIds[i]] || [];
+   371	      for (var k = 0; k < nbrs.length; k++) {
+   372	        if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(inDegree, nbrs[k])) inDegree[nbrs[k]]++;
+   373	      }
+   374	    }
+   375	    var queue = [];
+   376	    for (i = 0; i < nodeIds.length; i++) if (inDegree[nodeIds[i]] === 0) queue.push(nodeIds[i]);
+   377	    queue.sort(_strcmp);
+   378	    var out = [];
+   379	    while (queue.length > 0) {
+   380	      var u = queue.shift();
+   381	      out.push(u);
+   382	      var nbrs2 = (adjacencyCausal[u] || []).slice().sort(_strcmp);
+   383	      for (var m = 0; m < nbrs2.length; m++) {
+   384	        var v = nbrs2[m];
+   385	        if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(inDegree, v)) {
+   386	          inDegree[v]--;
+   387	          if (inDegree[v] === 0) {
+   388	            var lo = 0, hi = queue.length;
+   389	            while (lo < hi) { var mid = (lo + hi) >>> 1; if (queue[mid] < v) lo = mid + 1; else hi = mid; }
+   390	            queue.splice(lo, 0, v);
+   391	          }
+   392	        }
+   393	      }
+   394	    }
+   395	    return out;
+   396	  }
+   397	
+   398	  // Materialise the final closed-shape sanitized graph (deep freeze every nested array/object).
+   399	  function _materializeGraph(o) {
+   400	    // Deep freeze correlationGroups (RN-05): freeze memberNodeIds BEFORE the enclosing group.
+   401	    var cgFrozen = o.correlationGroups.map(function (g) {
+   402	      return Object.freeze({
+   403	        correlationGroupId: g.correlationGroupId,
+   404	        memberNodeIds: Object.freeze(g.memberNodeIds.slice()),
+   405	        independenceWeight: g.independenceWeight,
+   406	      });
+   407	    });
+   408	    var dedupSummaryFrozen = Object.freeze({
+   409	      rejectedDuplicateIds: Object.freeze(o.dedup.rejectedDuplicateIds.slice()),
+   410	      rejectedSemanticDuplicates: Object.freeze(o.dedup.rejectedSemanticDuplicates.map(function (r) {
+   411	        return Object.freeze({
+   412	          fingerprint: r.fingerprint,
+   413	          keptNodeId: r.keptNodeId,
+   414	          droppedNodeId: r.droppedNodeId,
+   415	        });
+   416	      })),
+   417	      rejectedSourceReplays: Object.freeze(o.dedup.rejectedSourceReplays.map(function (r) { return Object.freeze(Object.assign({}, r)); })),
+   418	    });
+   419	    var graph = {
+   420	      schemaVersion: GRAPH_SCHEMA_VERSION,
+   421	      graphId: o.graphId,
+   422	      caseAssociation: Object.freeze({
+   423	        caseId: o.caseAssociation.caseId,
+   424	        sessionId: o.caseAssociation.sessionId,
+   425	        lapId: o.caseAssociation.lapId == null ? null : o.caseAssociation.lapId,
+   426	      }),
+   427	      sessionAssociation: Object.freeze({ sessionId: o.caseAssociation.sessionId }),
+   428	      nodes: Object.freeze(o.nodes.slice()),
+   429	      edges: Object.freeze(o.edges.slice()),
+   430	      topologicalOrder: Object.freeze(o.topologicalOrder.slice()),
+   431	      deduplicationSummary: dedupSummaryFrozen,
+   432	      correlationGroups: Object.freeze(cgFrozen),
+   433	      limitations: Object.freeze(o.limitations.slice()),
+   434	      cannotConclude: Object.freeze(o.cannotConclude.slice()),
+   435	      provenance: Object.freeze({
+   436	        builderVersion: SERVICE_VERSION,
+   437	        inputCount: o.provenance.inputCount,
+   438	        sanitizedCount: o.provenance.sanitizedCount,
+   439	        rejectedCount: o.provenance.rejectedCount,
+   440	        rejectedReasonsSummary: Object.freeze(Object.assign({}, o.provenance.rejectedReasonsSummary)),
+   441	      }),
+   442	      createdAt: o.createdAt,
+   443	      generationToken: o.generationToken,
+   444	      contextVersion: o.contextVersion,
+   445	    };
+   446	    return Object.freeze(graph);
+   447	  }
+   448	
+   449	  /**
+   450	   * buildEvidenceGraph(input, opts) — primary entry. See module header for the contract.
+   451	   */
+   452	  function buildEvidenceGraph(inputIn, optsIn) {
+   453	    try {
+   454	      // Step 1 — input envelope structural gate + TRUSTED SNAPSHOT (RN-01 closure).
+   455	      var env = _validateInputEnvelope(inputIn);
+   456	      if (!env.ok) return env;
+   457	      var caseAssociation = env.snapshot.caseAssociation;
+   458	      var generationToken = env.snapshot.generationToken;
+   459	      var contextVersion = env.snapshot.contextVersion;
+   460	      var rawEvidenceRef = env.snapshot.rawEvidenceRef;
+   461	      var inputCount = env.snapshot.rawEvidenceLength;
+   462	
+   463	      // Step 2 — resolve clock AFTER envelope validation (RN-06 closure).
+   464	      var createdAt = _resolveClock(optsIn);
+   465	
+   466	      // Step 3 — per-node validation. Each rawEvidence[i] is read via descriptor (RN-02 closure):
+   467	      // accessor / sparse / inherited slots become ONE rejected node, never a whole-build throw.
+   468	      var sanitized = [];
+   469	      var rejectedCount = 0;
+   470	      var rejectedReasons = Object.create(null);
+   471	      function tally(code) { rejectedReasons[code] = (rejectedReasons[code] || 0) + 1; }
+   472	
+   473	      for (var i = 0; i < inputCount; i++) {
+   474	        var desc;
+   475	        try { desc = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(rawEvidenceRef, i); }
+   476	        catch (e) { rejectedCount++; tally(CODES.PROTOTYPE_POLLUTION_REJECTED); continue; }
+   477	        if (!desc) { rejectedCount++; tally(CODES.PROTOTYPE_POLLUTION_REJECTED); continue; }
+   478	        if (!('value' in desc)) { rejectedCount++; tally(CODES.PROTOTYPE_POLLUTION_REJECTED); continue; }
+   479	        var raw = desc.value;
+   480	        // outer defence — refuse non-plain prototype BEFORE invoking D1 validator.
+   481	        if (!RC.isOriginalPlainObject(raw)) { rejectedCount++; tally(CODES.PROTOTYPE_POLLUTION_REJECTED); continue; }
+   482	        if (RC.hasHiddenOwnKey(raw)) { rejectedCount++; tally(CODES.UNKNOWN_OWN_KEY); continue; }
+   483	        if (RC.hasNonPlainNestedObject(raw)) { rejectedCount++; tally(CODES.PROTOTYPE_POLLUTION_REJECTED); continue; }
+   484	        var check = EN.validateEvidenceNodeShape(raw);
+   485	        if (check.valid !== true) {
+   486	          rejectedCount++;
+   487	          var codes = Array.isArray(check.reasonCodes) ? check.reasonCodes : [CODES.EVIDENCE_NODE_INVALID];
+   488	          for (var rci = 0; rci < codes.length; rci++) tally(codes[rci]);
+   489	          continue;
+   490	        }
+   491	        var san = check.sanitized;
+   492	        // RN-09 — per-observation params key count cap (defence layer above D1).
+   493	        if (san.observation && san.observation.params && typeof san.observation.params === 'object') {
+   494	          try {
+   495	            var pkc = Object.keys(san.observation.params).length;
+   496	            if (pkc > PARAMS_KEYS_CAP) { rejectedCount++; tally(CODES.ARRAY_CAP_EXCEEDED); continue; }
+   497	          } catch (e) { rejectedCount++; tally(CODES.PROTOTYPE_POLLUTION_REJECTED); continue; }
+   498	        }
+   499	        // Association binding — caseId / sessionId MUST equal envelope.caseAssociation.
+   500	        if (san.identity.caseId !== caseAssociation.caseId) { rejectedCount++; tally(CODES.SOURCE_IDENTITY_CASE_MISMATCH); continue; }
+   501	        if (san.identity.sessionId !== caseAssociation.sessionId) { rejectedCount++; tally(CODES.SOURCE_IDENTITY_SESSION_MISMATCH); continue; }
+   502	        if (caseAssociation.lapId !== null && san.identity.lapId !== null && san.identity.lapId !== caseAssociation.lapId) {
+   503	          rejectedCount++; tally(CODES.SOURCE_IDENTITY_LAP_MISMATCH); continue;
+   504	        }
+   505	        // Imported-summary elevation guard.
+   506	        if (san.identity.sourceId === IMPORTED_SUMMARY_SOURCE_ID && san.credibility === 'measured') {
+   507	          rejectedCount++; tally(CODES.EVIDENCE_IMPORTED_SUMMARY_ELEVATION_FORBIDDEN); continue;
+   508	        }
+   509	        sanitized.push(san);
+   510	      }
+   511	
+   512	      // Step 4 — cap on accepted nodes.
+   513	      if (sanitized.length > NODES_CAP) {
+   514	        return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.GRAPH_CAP_EXCEEDED], { detail: 'sanitized node count ' + sanitized.length + ' exceeds NODES_CAP=' + NODES_CAP });
+   515	      }
+   516	
+   517	      // Step 5 — RN-03 closure: sort sanitized by full canonical content BEFORE any dedup so caller
+   518	      // permutation cannot influence which duplicate wins. Tie-breaker on a deterministic field.
+   519	      sanitized.sort(function (a, b) { return _strcmp(_fullCanonicalString(a), _fullCanonicalString(b)); });
+   520	
+   521	      // Step 6 — duplicate-ID dedup (Object.create(null) map — RN-08 closure). First by sort order
+   522	      // (deterministic) is kept; subsequent rejected.
+   523	      var byId = Object.create(null);
+   524	      var keptById = [];
+   525	      var rejectedDuplicateIds = [];
+   526	      for (var d = 0; d < sanitized.length; d++) {
+   527	        var n = sanitized[d];
+   528	        if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(byId, n.nodeId)) {
+   529	          rejectedDuplicateIds.push(n.nodeId);
+   530	          tally(CODES.EVIDENCE_DUPLICATE_ID);
+   531	          rejectedCount++;
+   532	          continue;
+   533	        }
+   534	        byId[n.nodeId] = n;
+   535	        keptById.push(n);
+   536	      }
+   537	      rejectedDuplicateIds.sort(_strcmp);
+   538	
+   539	      // Step 7 — semantic CANONICAL STRING dedup (RN-04 closure). The map KEY is the full canonical
+   540	      // string itself, not a hash. Collisions are impossible by definition.
+   541	      var semSeen = Object.create(null);
+   542	      var keptAfterSemantic = [];
+   543	      var rejectedSemanticDuplicates = [];
+   544	      for (var s = 0; s < keptById.length; s++) {
+   545	        var nn = keptById[s];
+   546	        var canon = _semanticCanonicalString(nn);
+   547	        if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(semSeen, canon)) {
+   548	          rejectedSemanticDuplicates.push({ fingerprint: _hash64('sem|' + canon), keptNodeId: semSeen[canon], droppedNodeId: nn.nodeId });
+   549	          tally(CODES.EVIDENCE_GRAPH_DUPLICATED_SOURCE_DOUBLECOUNT);
+   550	          rejectedCount++;
+   551	          delete byId[nn.nodeId];
+   552	          continue;
+   553	        }
+   554	        semSeen[canon] = nn.nodeId;
+   555	        keptAfterSemantic.push(nn);
+   556	      }
+   557	      rejectedSemanticDuplicates.sort(function (a, b) { return _strcmp(a.droppedNodeId, b.droppedNodeId); });
+   558	
+   559	      // Step 8 — orphan edge detection. Any supportingEdges / contradictingEdges target nodeId that
+   560	      // is not in the surviving graph triggers EVIDENCE_GRAPH_ORPHAN.
+   560	      // is not in the surviving graph triggers EVIDENCE_GRAPH_ORPHAN.
+   561	      var orphanDetected = false;
+   562	      var orphanDetail = null;
+   563	      for (var e = 0; e < keptAfterSemantic.length; e++) {
+   564	        var nx = keptAfterSemantic[e];
+   565	        var allEdges = nx.supportingEdges.concat(nx.contradictingEdges);
+   566	        for (var ek = 0; ek < allEdges.length; ek++) {
+   567	          var tgt = allEdges[ek];
+   568	          if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(byId, tgt)) {
+   569	            orphanDetected = true;
+   570	            orphanDetail = 'node ' + nx.nodeId + ' references unknown nodeId ' + tgt;
+   571	            break;
+   572	          }
+   573	        }
+   574	        if (orphanDetected) break;
+   575	      }
+   576	      if (orphanDetected) {
+   577	        return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_GRAPH_ORPHAN], { detail: orphanDetail });
+   578	      }
+   579	
+   580	      // Step 9 — build edges + causal adjacency (RN-08: proto-null map).
+   581	      var edges = [];
+   582	      var adjacencyCausal = Object.create(null);
+   583	      function addEdge(from, to, kind) {
+   584	        if (EDGE_KIND_ALLOWED.indexOf(kind) === -1) return false;
+   585	        edges.push({ from: from, to: to, kind: kind });
+   586	        if (EDGE_KIND_CAUSAL[kind]) {
+   587	          if (!adjacencyCausal[from]) adjacencyCausal[from] = [];
+   588	          adjacencyCausal[from].push(to);
+   589	        }
+   590	        return true;
+   591	      }
+   592	      for (var f = 0; f < keptAfterSemantic.length; f++) {
+   593	        var nf = keptAfterSemantic[f];
+   594	        for (var sf = 0; sf < nf.supportingEdges.length; sf++) {
+   595	          if (nf.supportingEdges[sf] === nf.nodeId) {
+   596	            return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_GRAPH_SELF_REFERENCE], { detail: nf.nodeId + ' supportingEdges self-reference' });
+   597	          }
+   598	          addEdge(nf.nodeId, nf.supportingEdges[sf], 'supports');
+   599	        }
+   600	        for (var cf = 0; cf < nf.contradictingEdges.length; cf++) {
+   601	          if (nf.contradictingEdges[cf] === nf.nodeId) {
+   602	            return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_GRAPH_SELF_REFERENCE], { detail: nf.nodeId + ' contradictingEdges self-reference' });
+   603	          }
+   604	          addEdge(nf.nodeId, nf.contradictingEdges[cf], 'contradicts');
+   605	        }
+   606	      }
+   607	
+   608	      // Step 10 — correlation groups. Same canonical (caseId|sessionId|lapId|sourceId) → one group.
+   609	      var groupBucket = Object.create(null);
+   610	      for (var g = 0; g < keptAfterSemantic.length; g++) {
+   611	        var ng = keptAfterSemantic[g];
+   612	        var gk = _correlationGroupCanonical(ng);
+   613	        if (!groupBucket[gk]) groupBucket[gk] = [];
+   614	        groupBucket[gk].push(ng.nodeId);
+   615	      }
+   616	      var groupKeysSorted = Object.keys(groupBucket).sort(_strcmp);
+   617	      var correlationGroups = [];
+   618	      for (var gi = 0; gi < groupKeysSorted.length; gi++) {
+   619	        var gk2 = groupKeysSorted[gi];
+   620	        var members = groupBucket[gk2].slice().sort(_strcmp);
+   621	        var iw = members.length > 0 ? 1 / members.length : 0;
+   622	        correlationGroups.push({
+   623	          correlationGroupId: _correlationGroupId(gk2),
+   624	          memberNodeIds: members,
+   625	          independenceWeight: iw,
+   626	        });
+   627	        if (members.length >= 2) {
+   628	          for (var mi = 0; mi < members.length; mi++) {
+   629	            for (var mj = mi + 1; mj < members.length; mj++) {
+   630	              var from = members[mi] < members[mj] ? members[mi] : members[mj];
+   631	              var to = members[mi] < members[mj] ? members[mj] : members[mi];
+   632	              addEdge(from, to, 'correlated_with');
+   633	            }
+   634	          }
+   635	        }
+   636	      }
+   637	      if (correlationGroups.length > CORRELATION_GROUPS_CAP) {
+   638	        return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.GRAPH_CAP_EXCEEDED], { detail: 'correlation groups ' + correlationGroups.length + ' exceeds CORRELATION_GROUPS_CAP=' + CORRELATION_GROUPS_CAP });
+   639	      }
+   640	
+   641	      // Step 11 — edge cap.
+   642	      if (edges.length > EDGES_CAP_TOTAL) {
+   643	        return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.GRAPH_CAP_EXCEEDED], { detail: 'edges ' + edges.length + ' exceeds EDGES_CAP_TOTAL=' + EDGES_CAP_TOTAL });
+   644	      }
+   645	
+   646	      // Step 12 — cycle detection over CAUSAL edges only.
+   647	      var sortedNodeIds = keptAfterSemantic.map(function (x) { return x.nodeId; }).slice().sort(_strcmp);
+   648	      var cycleHit = _findCycle(sortedNodeIds, adjacencyCausal);
+   649	      if (cycleHit !== null) {
+   650	        return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_GRAPH_CYCLE], { detail: 'cycle involves nodeId ' + cycleHit });
+   651	      }
+   652	
+   653	      // Step 13 — topological order over the acyclic causal subgraph.
+   654	      var topo = _topologicalOrder(sortedNodeIds, adjacencyCausal);
+   655	
+   656	      // Step 14 — sort nodes deterministically; sort edges by (from, to, kind).
+   657	      var nodesOut = keptAfterSemantic.slice().sort(function (a, b) { return _strcmp(a.nodeId, b.nodeId); });
+   658	      var edgesOut = edges.slice().sort(function (a, b) {
+   659	        var c = _strcmp(a.from, b.from); if (c !== 0) return c;
+   660	        c = _strcmp(a.to, b.to); if (c !== 0) return c;
+   661	        return _strcmp(a.kind, b.kind);
+   662	      });
+   663	      for (var ni = 0; ni < nodesOut.length; ni++) Object.freeze(nodesOut[ni]);
+   664	      for (var ei = 0; ei < edgesOut.length; ei++) Object.freeze(edgesOut[ei]);
+   665	
+   666	      // Step 15 — limitations / cannotConclude derivation.
+   667	      var limitations = [];
+   668	      var cannotConclude = [];
+   669	      var limSeen = Object.create(null);
+   670	      for (var li = 0; li < nodesOut.length; li++) {
+   671	        var lims = nodesOut[li].limitations;
+   672	        for (var lj = 0; lj < lims.length; lj++) {
+   673	          if (!limSeen[lims[lj]]) { limSeen[lims[lj]] = true; limitations.push(lims[lj]); }
+   674	        }
+   675	      }
+   676	      if (nodesOut.length === 0) {
+   677	        if (cannotConclude.indexOf(CODES.INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE) === -1) cannotConclude.push(CODES.INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE);
+   678	      }
+   679	      if (rejectedCount > 0) {
+   680	        if (cannotConclude.indexOf(CODES.CANNOT_CONCLUDE) === -1) cannotConclude.push(CODES.CANNOT_CONCLUDE);
+   681	      }
+   682	      limitations.sort(_strcmp);
+   683	      cannotConclude.sort(_strcmp);
+   684	      if (limitations.length > LIMITATIONS_CAP) limitations = limitations.slice(0, LIMITATIONS_CAP);
+   685	      if (cannotConclude.length > CANNOT_CONCLUDE_CAP) cannotConclude = cannotConclude.slice(0, CANNOT_CONCLUDE_CAP);
+   686	
+   687	      // Step 16 — derive graphId (content-only; clock-independent).
+   688	      var idPayload = {
+   689	        caseAssociation: caseAssociation,
+   690	        nodes: nodesOut.map(function (n) {
+   691	          return {
+   692	            nodeId: n.nodeId,
+   693	            category: n.category,
+   694	            credibility: n.credibility,
+   695	            provenance: n.provenance,
+   696	            availability: n.availability,
+   697	            identity: {
+   698	              caseId: n.identity.caseId,
+   699	              sessionId: n.identity.sessionId,
+   700	              lapId: n.identity.lapId,
+   701	              sourceId: n.identity.sourceId,
+   702	              sourceVersion: n.identity.sourceVersion,
+   703	            },
+   704	            observation: {
+   705	              kind: n.observation.kind,
+   706	              channel: n.observation.channel,
+   707	              i18nKey: n.observation.i18nKey,
+   708	              params: n.observation.params,
+   709	            },
+   710	            limitations: n.limitations,
+   711	            supportingEdges: n.supportingEdges,
+   712	            contradictingEdges: n.contradictingEdges,
+   713	          };
+   714	        }),
+   715	        edges: edgesOut,
+   716	        correlationGroups: correlationGroups.map(function (g) { return { correlationGroupId: g.correlationGroupId, memberNodeIds: g.memberNodeIds, independenceWeight: g.independenceWeight }; }),
+   717	      };
+   718	      var graphId = 'graph_' + _hash64('graphid|v' + GRAPH_SCHEMA_VERSION + '|' + _stableStringify(idPayload));
+   719	
+   720	      // Step 17 — RN-09 closure: post-sanitization JSON byte cap. Safe to JSON.stringify because every
+   721	      // sanitized value is a plain frozen primitive (no accessors, no Proxies, no class instances).
+   722	      var envBytes;
+   723	      try {
+   724	        var s = JSON.stringify(idPayload);
+   725	        envBytes = (typeof TextEncoder !== 'undefined') ? new TextEncoder().encode(s).length : Buffer.byteLength(s, 'utf8');
+   726	      } catch (e) { envBytes = Infinity; }
+   727	      if (envBytes > ENVELOPE_BYTE_CAP) {
+   728	        return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.BYTE_CAP_EXCEEDED], { detail: 'post-sanitization JSON size ' + envBytes + ' exceeds ENVELOPE_BYTE_CAP=' + ENVELOPE_BYTE_CAP });
+   729	      }
+   730	
+   731	      // Step 18 — final materialisation
+   732	      var graph = _materializeGraph({
+   733	        graphId: graphId,
+   734	        caseAssociation: caseAssociation,
+   735	        nodes: nodesOut,
+   736	        edges: edgesOut,
+   737	        topologicalOrder: topo,
+   738	        dedup: {
+   739	          rejectedDuplicateIds: rejectedDuplicateIds,
+   740	          rejectedSemanticDuplicates: rejectedSemanticDuplicates,
+   741	          rejectedSourceReplays: [],
+   742	        },
+   743	        correlationGroups: correlationGroups,
+   744	        limitations: limitations,
+   745	        cannotConclude: cannotConclude,
+   746	        provenance: {
+   747	          inputCount: inputCount,
+   748	          sanitizedCount: nodesOut.length,
+   749	          rejectedCount: rejectedCount,
+   750	          rejectedReasonsSummary: rejectedReasons,
+   751	        },
+   752	        createdAt: createdAt,
+   753	        generationToken: generationToken,
+   754	        contextVersion: contextVersion,
+   755	      });
+   756	
+   757	      return Object.freeze({ valid: true, graph: graph });
+   758	    } catch (e) {
+   759	      return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.INTERNAL_CONTRACT_VIOLATION], { detail: 'buildEvidenceGraph threw on hostile input' });
+   760	    }
+   761	  }
+   762	
+   763	  // Public API surface — RN-07 closure: internal helpers (_stableStringify, _semanticCanonicalString,
+   764	  // _correlationGroupCanonical) are NOT exported. The only public entry is buildEvidenceGraph plus
+   765	  // documented constants. Tests must drive the contract via buildEvidenceGraph only.
+   766	  var api = {
+   767	    SERVICE_VERSION: SERVICE_VERSION,
+   768	    GRAPH_SCHEMA_VERSION: GRAPH_SCHEMA_VERSION,
+   769	    EDGE_KIND_ALLOWED: EDGE_KIND_ALLOWED,
+   770	    NODES_CAP: NODES_CAP,
+   771	    EDGES_CAP_TOTAL: EDGES_CAP_TOTAL,
+   772	    CORRELATION_GROUPS_CAP: CORRELATION_GROUPS_CAP,
+   773	    INPUT_RAW_EVIDENCE_CAP: INPUT_RAW_EVIDENCE_CAP,
+   774	    PARAMS_KEYS_CAP: PARAMS_KEYS_CAP,
+   775	    ENVELOPE_BYTE_CAP: ENVELOPE_BYTE_CAP,
+   776	    IMPORTED_SUMMARY_SOURCE_ID: IMPORTED_SUMMARY_SOURCE_ID,
+   777	    IMPORTED_SUMMARY_MAX_CREDIBILITY: IMPORTED_SUMMARY_MAX_CREDIBILITY,
+   778	    buildEvidenceGraph: buildEvidenceGraph,
+   779	  };
+   780	  if (typeof module !== 'undefined' && module.exports) module.exports = api;
+   781	  if (root) root.R3_0D_EvidenceGraph = api;
+   782	})(typeof globalThis !== 'undefined' ? globalThis : this);
+     1	#!/usr/bin/env node
+     2	'use strict';
+     3	/**
+     4	 * R3.0 Integrated Delivery Train — per-phase production-consumer authorization validator (D / E / F).
+     5	 *
+     6	 * Parameterized variant of scripts/check-r3-0c-no-consumer.js (which guards R3.0C).
+     7	 * The phase to validate is selected by env R3_PHASE_PROGRAM (one of 'R3.0D', 'R3.0E', 'R3.0F').
+     8	 *
+     9	 * Inspects the CURRENT WORKING-TREE STATE: confirms that EVERY production code consumer of the
+    10	 * phase's contract directory (e.g. contracts/r3.0d/ for R3.0D) is explicitly listed in
+    11	 * governance/<phase-dir>/state.json :: authorizedProductionPaths. Specifically:
+    12	 *
+    13	 *   1. Every renderer/js/*.js file that requires/imports anything under contracts/<phase>/
+    14	 *      MUST be listed in state.authorizedProductionPaths.
+    15	 *   2. main.js / preload.js consumers MUST be similarly authorized.
+    16	 *   3. renderer/index.html script src pointing into contracts/<phase>/ is ALWAYS forbidden
+    17	 *      (contracts are UMD modules consumed via require/import, never loaded as scripts).
+    18	 *
+    19	 * Tests under tests/ and docs under docs/ ARE allowed to reference contracts/<phase>/ freely.
+    20	 *
+    21	 * Authorization gate behaviour:
+    22	 *   • At the bootstrap / contracts-only checkpoint (e.g. D1_CONTRACT_FOUNDATION),
+    23	 *     state.authorizedProductionPaths is empty → ANY consumer fails as PROD_UNAUTHORIZED_CONSUMER.
+    24	 *   • At a production checkpoint (e.g. D2_EVIDENCE_GRAPH), the authorized list contains the
+    25	 *     specific renderer/js files allowed to require contracts. Consumers on the list PASS;
+    26	 *     consumers off the list FAIL as PROD_UNAUTHORIZED_CONSUMER. The presence of authorization
+    27	 *     does NOT weaken the check — it strictly defines the allow surface.
+    28	 *
+    29	 * Unconditional: runs every PR and every push to main. Internal exceptions fail closed (ok=false, exit 2).
+    30	 *
+    31	 * Output: ${ARTIFACT_DIR:-artifacts}/r3-<lower-dashed-program>-no-consumer.json
+    32	 */
+    33	const fs = require('fs');
+    34	const path = require('path');
+    35	
+    36	const REPO = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+    37	const ARTIFACT_DIR = process.env.ARTIFACT_DIR ? path.resolve(process.env.ARTIFACT_DIR) : path.join(REPO, 'artifacts');
+    38	
+    39	const PHASE_CONFIG = {
+    40	  'R3.0D': { contractPrefix: 'contracts/r3.0d', artifact: 'r3-0d-no-consumer.json', label: 'R3.0D-NOCONSUMER', stateDir: 'governance/r3.0d' },
+    41	  'R3.0E': { contractPrefix: 'contracts/r3.0e', artifact: 'r3-0e-no-consumer.json', label: 'R3.0E-NOCONSUMER', stateDir: 'governance/r3.0e' },
+    42	  'R3.0F': { contractPrefix: 'contracts/r3.0f', artifact: 'r3-0f-no-consumer.json', label: 'R3.0F-NOCONSUMER', stateDir: 'governance/r3.0f' },
+    43	};
+    44	
+    45	const PHASE_PROGRAM = String(process.env.R3_PHASE_PROGRAM || '').trim();
+    46	const PHASE = PHASE_CONFIG[PHASE_PROGRAM];
+    47	
+    48	const SCAN_BASE = process.env.R3_PHASE_NO_CONSUMER_BASE_OVERRIDE ? path.resolve(process.env.R3_PHASE_NO_CONSUMER_BASE_OVERRIDE) : REPO;
+    49	const CONTRACT_PREFIX = PHASE ? PHASE.contractPrefix : 'contracts/__unknown__';
+    50	
+    51	const REQUIRE_STR_RE = /(?:require|import)\s*\(\s*(['"`])((?:[^'"`\\]|\\.)*)\1\s*\)/g;
+    52	const IMPORT_FROM_RE = /import\s+(?:[^'"`]+?\s+from\s+)?(['"`])((?:[^'"`\\]|\\.)*)\1/g;
+    53	
+    54	function listJsFiles(dir) {
+    55	  const out = [];
+    56	  let entries;
+    57	  try { entries = fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true }); }
+    58	  catch (_) { return out; }
+    59	  for (const e of entries) {
+    60	    const full = path.join(dir, e.name);
+    61	    if (e.isDirectory()) out.push.apply(out, listJsFiles(full));
+    62	    else if (e.isFile() && full.endsWith('.js')) out.push(full);
+    63	  }
+    64	  return out;
+    65	}
+    66	
+    67	function resolvedTargetIfContract(fromFile, spec) {
+    68	  if (typeof spec !== 'string' || spec.length === 0) return null;
+    69	  if (!(spec.startsWith('./') || spec.startsWith('../') || spec === '.' || spec === '..')) return null;
+    70	  const abs = path.resolve(path.dirname(fromFile), spec);
+    71	  const rel = path.relative(SCAN_BASE, abs).split(path.sep).join('/');
+    72	  if (rel === CONTRACT_PREFIX || rel.startsWith(CONTRACT_PREFIX + '/')) return rel;
+    73	  return null;
+    74	}
+    75	
+    76	function extractRequireSpecs(src) {
+    77	  const specs = [];
+    78	  let m;
+    79	  REQUIRE_STR_RE.lastIndex = 0;
+    80	  while ((m = REQUIRE_STR_RE.exec(src)) !== null) specs.push(m[2]);
+    81	  IMPORT_FROM_RE.lastIndex = 0;
+    82	  while ((m = IMPORT_FROM_RE.exec(src)) !== null) specs.push(m[2]);
+    83	  return specs;
+    84	}
+    85	
+    86	function scanFile(file, violations, authorizedSet, allConsumers) {
+    87	  let src;
+    88	  try { src = fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8'); }
+    89	  catch (e) { violations.push({ code: 'FILE_UNREADABLE', file: path.relative(REPO, file), message: e.message }); return; }
+    90	  const specs = extractRequireSpecs(src);
+    91	  const relFile = path.relative(SCAN_BASE === REPO ? REPO : SCAN_BASE, file).split(path.sep).join('/');
+    92	  for (const spec of specs) {
+    93	    let isConsumer = false;
+    94	    let specifierForReport = null;
+    95	    let targetForReport = null;
+    96	    if (spec.indexOf(CONTRACT_PREFIX) !== -1) {
+    97	      isConsumer = true;
+    98	      specifierForReport = spec;
+    99	    } else {
+   100	      const target = resolvedTargetIfContract(file, spec);
+   101	      if (target) { isConsumer = true; specifierForReport = spec; targetForReport = target; }
+   102	    }
+   103	    if (!isConsumer) continue;
+   104	    allConsumers.push({ file: relFile, specifier: specifierForReport, target: targetForReport });
+   105	    if (!authorizedSet.has(relFile)) {
+   106	      const v = { code: 'PROD_UNAUTHORIZED_CONSUMER', file: relFile, specifier: specifierForReport, phase: PHASE_PROGRAM };
+   107	      if (targetForReport) v.target = targetForReport;
+   108	      violations.push(v);
+   109	    }
+   110	  }
+   111	  // RN-11 closure: secondary string-concat-near-require heuristic. ONLY emits when the file is
+   112	  // NOT in the authorized set — an authorized consumer's UMD require is the expected case.
+   113	  if (!authorizedSet.has(relFile) && detectSuspectedDynamicPhaseRequire(src)) {
+   114	    violations.push({ code: 'PROD_SUSPECTED_DYNAMIC_PHASE_REQUIRE', file: relFile, phase: PHASE_PROGRAM });
+   115	  }
+   116	}
+   117	
+   118	function scanIndexHtml(violations) {
+   119	  const file = path.join(SCAN_BASE, 'renderer', 'index.html');
+   120	  let src;
+   121	  try { src = fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8'); }
+   122	  catch (e) { violations.push({ code: 'INDEX_HTML_UNREADABLE', message: e.message }); return; }
+   123	  const scriptRe = /<script[^>]*\bsrc\s*=\s*(['"])([^'"]+)\1/gi;
+   124	  let m;
+   125	  while ((m = scriptRe.exec(src)) !== null) {
+   126	    const s = m[2];
+   127	    if (s.indexOf(CONTRACT_PREFIX) !== -1) violations.push({ code: 'INDEX_HTML_SCRIPT_LOADS_CONTRACTS', src: s, phase: PHASE_PROGRAM });
+   128	  }
+   129	}
+   130	
+   131	function finish(payload) {
+   132	  return Object.assign({ check: PHASE ? ('r3-' + PHASE_PROGRAM.toLowerCase().replace('.', '-') + '-no-consumer').replace('--', '-') : 'r3-phase-no-consumer', program: PHASE_PROGRAM || null }, payload);
+   133	}
+   134	
+   135	// Codex D2 Round 1 RN-10 closure: strict entry-shape validation. Any malformed entry fails closed
+   136	// (allow set becomes empty AND a STATE_AUTHORIZED_PATH_ENTRY_INVALID violation is emitted, which
+   137	// makes the whole check fail). Validates exact key set, path grammar (repo-relative, no '..', no
+   138	// '/' prefix, no glob chars, conservative segment regex), capability non-empty string, and rejects
+   139	// duplicates.
+   140	const SAFE_SEGMENT_RE = /^[A-Za-z0-9_.-]+$/;
+   141	function _validatePathString(p) {
+   142	  if (typeof p !== 'string' || p.length === 0) return 'empty';
+   143	  if (p.startsWith('/')) return 'absolute';
+   144	  if (p.includes('..')) return 'parent-segment';
+   145	  if (p !== p.trim()) return 'whitespace';
+   146	  if (/[\r\n\t]/.test(p)) return 'control-char';
+   147	  if (/[*?[\]{}()|\\^$#~]/.test(p)) return 'glob-or-regex-char';
+   148	  const segments = p.split('/');
+   149	  for (const seg of segments) {
+   150	    if (seg.length === 0) return 'empty-segment';
+   151	    if (!SAFE_SEGMENT_RE.test(seg)) return 'invalid-segment-char';
+   152	  }
+   153	  return null;
+   154	}
+   155	const AUTHORIZED_ENTRY_KEYS = new Set(['path', 'capability']);
+   156	function loadAuthorizedSet() {
+   157	  if (!PHASE) return { set: new Set(), violations: [] };
+   158	  const stateOverride = process.env.R3_PHASE_STATE_FILE_OVERRIDE ? path.resolve(process.env.R3_PHASE_STATE_FILE_OVERRIDE) : null;
+   159	  const statePath = stateOverride || path.join(REPO, PHASE.stateDir, 'state.json');
+   160	  let raw;
+   161	  try { raw = fs.readFileSync(statePath, 'utf8'); }
+   162	  catch (e) { return { set: new Set(), violations: [{ code: 'STATE_UNREADABLE', message: statePath + ': ' + e.message }] }; }
+   163	  let st;
+   164	  try { st = JSON.parse(raw); }
+   165	  catch (e) { return { set: new Set(), violations: [{ code: 'STATE_PARSE_ERROR', message: statePath + ': ' + e.message }] }; }
+   166	  if (!st || typeof st !== 'object' || Array.isArray(st)) return { set: new Set(), violations: [{ code: 'STATE_AUTHORIZED_PATHS_MALFORMED', message: 'state.json root is not a plain object' }] };
+   167	  if (!Array.isArray(st.authorizedProductionPaths)) return { set: new Set(), violations: [{ code: 'STATE_AUTHORIZED_PATHS_MALFORMED', message: 'authorizedProductionPaths missing or not an array' }] };
+   168	  const set = new Set();
+   169	  const violations = [];
+   170	  const seenPaths = new Set();
+   171	  for (let i = 0; i < st.authorizedProductionPaths.length; i++) {
+   172	    const entry = st.authorizedProductionPaths[i];
+   173	    if (entry === null || typeof entry !== 'object' || Array.isArray(entry)) {
+   174	      violations.push({ code: 'STATE_AUTHORIZED_PATH_ENTRY_INVALID', index: i, reason: 'not-a-plain-object' });
+   175	      continue;
+   176	    }
+   177	    // Exact closed key set: must have BOTH path AND capability; no extra keys (RN-10 closure).
+   178	    let extraKey = null;
+   179	    for (const k of Object.keys(entry)) if (!AUTHORIZED_ENTRY_KEYS.has(k)) { extraKey = k; break; }
+   180	    if (extraKey) {
+   181	      violations.push({ code: 'STATE_AUTHORIZED_PATH_ENTRY_INVALID', index: i, reason: 'unknown-key:' + extraKey });
+   182	      continue;
+   183	    }
+   184	    if (!('path' in entry) || !('capability' in entry)) {
+   185	      violations.push({ code: 'STATE_AUTHORIZED_PATH_ENTRY_INVALID', index: i, reason: 'missing-required-key' });
+   186	      continue;
+   187	    }
+   188	    if (typeof entry.path !== 'string' || entry.path.length === 0) {
+   189	      violations.push({ code: 'STATE_AUTHORIZED_PATH_ENTRY_INVALID', index: i, reason: 'path-not-string' });
+   190	      continue;
+   191	    }
+   192	    if (typeof entry.capability !== 'string' || entry.capability.length === 0) {
+   193	      violations.push({ code: 'STATE_AUTHORIZED_PATH_ENTRY_INVALID', index: i, path: entry.path, reason: 'capability-not-string' });
+   194	      continue;
+   195	    }
+   196	    const pathReason = _validatePathString(entry.path);
+   197	    if (pathReason) {
+   198	      violations.push({ code: 'STATE_AUTHORIZED_PATH_GRAMMAR_INVALID', index: i, path: entry.path, reason: pathReason });
+   199	      continue;
+   200	    }
+   201	    if (seenPaths.has(entry.path)) {
+   202	      violations.push({ code: 'STATE_AUTHORIZED_PATH_DUPLICATE', index: i, path: entry.path });
+   203	      continue;
+   204	    }
+   205	    seenPaths.add(entry.path);
+   206	    set.add(entry.path);
+   207	  }
+   208	  // RN-10 closure: if ANY entry-level violation occurred, the allow set is treated as EMPTY so a
+   209	  // partially-malformed state cannot smuggle through a single good entry that authorizes anything.
+   210	  if (violations.length > 0) return { set: new Set(), violations };
+   211	  return { set, violations: [] };
+   212	}
+   213	
+   214	// Codex D2 Round 1 RN-11 closure: detect string-concatenation-near-require shapes that would
+   215	// resolve to a phase contract path. Heuristic: a require/import call with a non-literal first
+   216	// argument AND the file contains a literal `r3.0d` / `r3.0e` / `r3.0f` substring. Flag as a
+   217	// PROD_SUSPECTED_DYNAMIC_PHASE_REQUIRE finding. Documented limitation: AST-level constant
+   218	// folding is out of scope; the heuristic errs on the side of caution and may produce false
+   219	// positives that future code may need to refactor (this is the intended fail-closed posture).
+   220	const NONLITERAL_REQUIRE_RE = /(?:require|import)\s*\(\s*(?![\s'"`])/g;
+   221	const PHASE_SUBSTR_RE = /\br3\.0[def]\b/i;
+   222	function detectSuspectedDynamicPhaseRequire(src) {
+   223	  if (!PHASE_SUBSTR_RE.test(src)) return false;
+   224	  NONLITERAL_REQUIRE_RE.lastIndex = 0;
+   225	  return NONLITERAL_REQUIRE_RE.test(src);
+   226	}
+   227	
+   228	function run() {
+   229	  if (!PHASE) {
+   230	    return finish({ ok: false, violations: [{ code: 'PHASE_PROGRAM_INVALID', message: 'R3_PHASE_PROGRAM must be one of R3.0D / R3.0E / R3.0F; got ' + JSON.stringify(PHASE_PROGRAM) }], productionConsumerCount: -1, runtimeConsumerCount: -1, authorizedPathCount: -1 });
+   231	  }
+   232	
+   233	  const violations = [];
+   234	  const allConsumers = [];
+   235	  const { set: authorizedSet, violations: stateViolations } = loadAuthorizedSet();
+   236	  for (const v of stateViolations) violations.push(v);
+   237	
+   238	  const rendererJs = listJsFiles(path.join(SCAN_BASE, 'renderer', 'js'));
+   239	  for (const f of rendererJs) scanFile(f, violations, authorizedSet, allConsumers);
+   240	
+   241	  for (const entry of ['main.js', 'preload.js']) {
+   242	    const full = path.join(SCAN_BASE, entry);
+   243	    if (fs.existsSync(full)) scanFile(full, violations, authorizedSet, allConsumers);
+   244	  }
+   245	
+   246	  scanIndexHtml(violations);
+   247	
+   248	  const unauthorizedConsumerCount = violations.filter(v => v.code === 'PROD_UNAUTHORIZED_CONSUMER' || v.code === 'INDEX_HTML_SCRIPT_LOADS_CONTRACTS').length;
+   249	  const authorizedConsumerCount = allConsumers.length - unauthorizedConsumerCount;
+   250	
+   251	  return finish({
+   252	    scanBase: SCAN_BASE === REPO ? '<repo>' : SCAN_BASE,
+   253	    contractPrefix: CONTRACT_PREFIX,
+   254	    scannedFiles: rendererJs.length + (fs.existsSync(path.join(SCAN_BASE, 'main.js')) ? 1 : 0) + (fs.existsSync(path.join(SCAN_BASE, 'preload.js')) ? 1 : 0) + 1,
+   255	    authorizedPathCount: authorizedSet.size,
+   256	    productionConsumerCount: allConsumers.length,
+   257	    authorizedConsumerCount: authorizedConsumerCount,
+   258	    unauthorizedConsumerCount: unauthorizedConsumerCount,
+   259	    runtimeConsumerCount: allConsumers.length,
+   260	    violations,
+   261	    ok: violations.length === 0,
+   262	  });
+   263	}
+   264	
+   265	let result, exitCode;
+   266	try { result = run(); exitCode = result.ok ? 0 : 1; }
+   267	catch (e) {
+   268	  result = finish({
+   269	    ok: false,
+   270	    productionConsumerCount: -1,
+   271	    runtimeConsumerCount: -1,
+   272	    authorizedPathCount: -1,
+   273	    violations: [{ code: 'INTERNAL_VALIDATOR_FAILURE', message: String((e && e.stack) || e) }],
+   274	  });
+   275	  exitCode = 2;
+   276	}
+   277	fs.mkdirSync(ARTIFACT_DIR, { recursive: true });
+   278	const outName = PHASE ? PHASE.artifact : 'r3-phase-no-consumer.json';
+   279	fs.writeFileSync(path.join(ARTIFACT_DIR, outName), JSON.stringify(result, null, 2));
+   280	const label = PHASE ? PHASE.label : 'R3-PHASE-NOCONSUMER';
+   281	console.log(label + ' ' + JSON.stringify({
+   282	  program: result.program,
+   283	  scanned: result.scannedFiles,
+   284	  authorizedPaths: result.authorizedPathCount,
+   285	  consumers: result.runtimeConsumerCount,
+   286	  authorizedConsumers: result.authorizedConsumerCount,
+   287	  unauthorizedConsumers: result.unauthorizedConsumerCount,
+   288	  violations: (result.violations || []).length,
+   289	  ok: result.ok,
+   290	}));

+   312	  // RN-06 closure: resolve opts.clock AFTER envelope validation; descriptor-safe; throwing getter
+   313	  // collapses to createdAt = null (clock is best-effort; never blocks the build).
+   314	  function _resolveClock(opts) {
+   315	    if (opts === null || opts === undefined) return null;
+   316	    if (!RC.isOriginalPlainObject(opts)) return null;
+   317	    if (RC.hasHiddenOwnKey(opts)) return null;
+   318	    if (!_hasOnlyAllowedKeys(opts, OPTS_KEYS)) return null;
+   319	    var desc;
+   320	    try { desc = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(opts, 'clock'); }
+   321	    catch (e) { return null; }
+   322	    if (!desc) return null;
+   323	    if (!('value' in desc)) return null;       // accessor descriptor — fail-soft
+   324	    var clock = desc.value;
+   325	    if (typeof clock !== 'function') return null;
+   326	    try {
+   327	      var v = clock();
+   328	      if (v === null) return null;
+   329	      if (!_isIsoTimestamp(v)) return null;
+   330	      return v;
+   331	    } catch (e) { return null; }
+   332	  }
+   333	
+   334	  // DFS cycle detection over the directed CAUSAL edge list. Uses a proto-null colour map (RN-08).
+   335	  function _findCycle(nodeIds, adjacencyCausal) {
+   336	    var WHITE = 0, GREY = 1, BLACK = 2;
+   337	    var color = Object.create(null);
+   338	    for (var i = 0; i < nodeIds.length; i++) color[nodeIds[i]] = WHITE;
+   339	    function visit(u) {
+   340	      color[u] = GREY;
+   341	      var nbrs = adjacencyCausal[u] || [];
+   342	      for (var k = 0; k < nbrs.length; k++) {
+   343	        var v = nbrs[k];
+   344	        if (color[v] === GREY) return v;
+   345	        if (color[v] === WHITE) {
+   346	          var c = visit(v);
+   347	          if (c !== null) return c;
+   348	        }
+   349	      }
+   350	      color[u] = BLACK;
+   351	      return null;
+   352	    }
+   353	    for (var j = 0; j < nodeIds.length; j++) {
+   354	      var n = nodeIds[j];
+   355	      if (color[n] === WHITE) {
+   356	        var hit = visit(n);
+   357	        if (hit !== null) return hit;
+   358	      }
+   359	    }
+   360	    return null;
+   361	  }
+   362	
+   363	  // Kahn's algorithm — deterministic by tie-breaking on sorted nodeId. Assumes no cycle. Uses
+   364	  // proto-null in-degree map (RN-08).
+   365	  function _topologicalOrder(nodeIds, adjacencyCausal) {
+   366	    var inDegree = Object.create(null);
+   367	    var i;
+   368	    for (i = 0; i < nodeIds.length; i++) inDegree[nodeIds[i]] = 0;
+   369	    for (i = 0; i < nodeIds.length; i++) {
+   370	      var nbrs = adjacencyCausal[nodeIds[i]] || [];
+   371	      for (var k = 0; k < nbrs.length; k++) {
+   372	        if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(inDegree, nbrs[k])) inDegree[nbrs[k]]++;
+   373	      }
+   374	    }
+   375	    var queue = [];
+   376	    for (i = 0; i < nodeIds.length; i++) if (inDegree[nodeIds[i]] === 0) queue.push(nodeIds[i]);
+   377	    queue.sort(_strcmp);
+   378	    var out = [];
+   379	    while (queue.length > 0) {
+   380	      var u = queue.shift();
+   381	      out.push(u);
+   382	      var nbrs2 = (adjacencyCausal[u] || []).slice().sort(_strcmp);
+   383	      for (var m = 0; m < nbrs2.length; m++) {
+   384	        var v = nbrs2[m];
+   385	        if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(inDegree, v)) {
+   386	          inDegree[v]--;
+   387	          if (inDegree[v] === 0) {
+   388	            var lo = 0, hi = queue.length;
+   389	            while (lo < hi) { var mid = (lo + hi) >>> 1; if (queue[mid] < v) lo = mid + 1; else hi = mid; }
+   390	            queue.splice(lo, 0, v);
+   391	          }
+   392	        }
+   393	      }
+   394	    }
+   395	    return out;
+   396	  }
+   397	
+   398	  // Materialise the final closed-shape sanitized graph (deep freeze every nested array/object).
+   399	  function _materializeGraph(o) {
+   400	    // Deep freeze correlationGroups (RN-05): freeze memberNodeIds BEFORE the enclosing group.
+   401	    var cgFrozen = o.correlationGroups.map(function (g) {
+   402	      return Object.freeze({
+   403	        correlationGroupId: g.correlationGroupId,
+   404	        memberNodeIds: Object.freeze(g.memberNodeIds.slice()),
+   405	        independenceWeight: g.independenceWeight,
+   406	      });
+   407	    });
+   408	    var dedupSummaryFrozen = Object.freeze({
+   409	      rejectedDuplicateIds: Object.freeze(o.dedup.rejectedDuplicateIds.slice()),
+   410	      rejectedSemanticDuplicates: Object.freeze(o.dedup.rejectedSemanticDuplicates.map(function (r) {
+   411	        return Object.freeze({
+   412	          fingerprint: r.fingerprint,
+   413	          keptNodeId: r.keptNodeId,
+   414	          droppedNodeId: r.droppedNodeId,
+   415	        });
+   416	      })),
+   417	      rejectedSourceReplays: Object.freeze(o.dedup.rejectedSourceReplays.map(function (r) { return Object.freeze(Object.assign({}, r)); })),
+   418	    });
+   419	    var graph = {
+   420	      schemaVersion: GRAPH_SCHEMA_VERSION,
+   421	      graphId: o.graphId,
+   422	      caseAssociation: Object.freeze({
+   423	        caseId: o.caseAssociation.caseId,
+   424	        sessionId: o.caseAssociation.sessionId,
+   425	        lapId: o.caseAssociation.lapId == null ? null : o.caseAssociation.lapId,
+   426	      }),
+   427	      sessionAssociation: Object.freeze({ sessionId: o.caseAssociation.sessionId }),
+   428	      nodes: Object.freeze(o.nodes.slice()),
+   429	      edges: Object.freeze(o.edges.slice()),
+   430	      topologicalOrder: Object.freeze(o.topologicalOrder.slice()),
+   431	      deduplicationSummary: dedupSummaryFrozen,
+   432	      correlationGroups: Object.freeze(cgFrozen),
+   433	      limitations: Object.freeze(o.limitations.slice()),
+   434	      cannotConclude: Object.freeze(o.cannotConclude.slice()),
+   435	      provenance: Object.freeze({
+   436	        builderVersion: SERVICE_VERSION,
+   437	        inputCount: o.provenance.inputCount,
+   438	        sanitizedCount: o.provenance.sanitizedCount,
+   439	        rejectedCount: o.provenance.rejectedCount,
+   440	        rejectedReasonsSummary: Object.freeze(Object.assign({}, o.provenance.rejectedReasonsSummary)),
+   441	      }),
+   442	      createdAt: o.createdAt,
+   443	      generationToken: o.generationToken,
+   444	      contextVersion: o.contextVersion,
+   445	    };
+   446	    return Object.freeze(graph);
+   447	  }
+   448	
+   449	  /**
+   450	   * buildEvidenceGraph(input, opts) — primary entry. See module header for the contract.
+   451	   */
+   452	  function buildEvidenceGraph(inputIn, optsIn) {
+   453	    try {
+   454	      // Step 1 — input envelope structural gate + TRUSTED SNAPSHOT (RN-01 closure).
+   455	      var env = _validateInputEnvelope(inputIn);
+   456	      if (!env.ok) return env;
+   457	      var caseAssociation = env.snapshot.caseAssociation;
+   458	      var generationToken = env.snapshot.generationToken;
+   459	      var contextVersion = env.snapshot.contextVersion;
+   460	      var rawEvidenceRef = env.snapshot.rawEvidenceRef;
+   461	      var inputCount = env.snapshot.rawEvidenceLength;
+   462	
+   463	      // Step 2 — resolve clock AFTER envelope validation (RN-06 closure).
+   464	      var createdAt = _resolveClock(optsIn);
+   465	
+   466	      // Step 3 — per-node validation. Each rawEvidence[i] is read via descriptor (RN-02 closure):
+   467	      // accessor / sparse / inherited slots become ONE rejected node, never a whole-build throw.
+   468	      var sanitized = [];
+   469	      var rejectedCount = 0;
+   470	      var rejectedReasons = Object.create(null);
+   471	      function tally(code) { rejectedReasons[code] = (rejectedReasons[code] || 0) + 1; }
+   472	
+   473	      for (var i = 0; i < inputCount; i++) {
+   474	        var desc;
+   475	        try { desc = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(rawEvidenceRef, i); }
+   476	        catch (e) { rejectedCount++; tally(CODES.PROTOTYPE_POLLUTION_REJECTED); continue; }
+   477	        if (!desc) { rejectedCount++; tally(CODES.PROTOTYPE_POLLUTION_REJECTED); continue; }
+   478	        if (!('value' in desc)) { rejectedCount++; tally(CODES.PROTOTYPE_POLLUTION_REJECTED); continue; }
+   479	        var raw = desc.value;
+   480	        // outer defence — refuse non-plain prototype BEFORE invoking D1 validator.
+   481	        if (!RC.isOriginalPlainObject(raw)) { rejectedCount++; tally(CODES.PROTOTYPE_POLLUTION_REJECTED); continue; }
+   482	        if (RC.hasHiddenOwnKey(raw)) { rejectedCount++; tally(CODES.UNKNOWN_OWN_KEY); continue; }
+   483	        if (RC.hasNonPlainNestedObject(raw)) { rejectedCount++; tally(CODES.PROTOTYPE_POLLUTION_REJECTED); continue; }
+   484	        var check = EN.validateEvidenceNodeShape(raw);
+   485	        if (check.valid !== true) {
+   486	          rejectedCount++;
+   487	          var codes = Array.isArray(check.reasonCodes) ? check.reasonCodes : [CODES.EVIDENCE_NODE_INVALID];
+   488	          for (var rci = 0; rci < codes.length; rci++) tally(codes[rci]);
+   489	          continue;
+   490	        }
+   491	        var san = check.sanitized;
+   492	        // RN-09 — per-observation params key count cap (defence layer above D1).
+   493	        if (san.observation && san.observation.params && typeof san.observation.params === 'object') {
+   494	          try {
+   495	            var pkc = Object.keys(san.observation.params).length;
+   496	            if (pkc > PARAMS_KEYS_CAP) { rejectedCount++; tally(CODES.ARRAY_CAP_EXCEEDED); continue; }
+   497	          } catch (e) { rejectedCount++; tally(CODES.PROTOTYPE_POLLUTION_REJECTED); continue; }
+   498	        }
+   499	        // Association binding — caseId / sessionId MUST equal envelope.caseAssociation.
+   500	        if (san.identity.caseId !== caseAssociation.caseId) { rejectedCount++; tally(CODES.SOURCE_IDENTITY_CASE_MISMATCH); continue; }
+   501	        if (san.identity.sessionId !== caseAssociation.sessionId) { rejectedCount++; tally(CODES.SOURCE_IDENTITY_SESSION_MISMATCH); continue; }
+   502	        if (caseAssociation.lapId !== null && san.identity.lapId !== null && san.identity.lapId !== caseAssociation.lapId) {
+   503	          rejectedCount++; tally(CODES.SOURCE_IDENTITY_LAP_MISMATCH); continue;
+   504	        }
+   505	        // Imported-summary elevation guard.
+   506	        if (san.identity.sourceId === IMPORTED_SUMMARY_SOURCE_ID && san.credibility === 'measured') {
+   507	          rejectedCount++; tally(CODES.EVIDENCE_IMPORTED_SUMMARY_ELEVATION_FORBIDDEN); continue;
+   508	        }
+   509	        sanitized.push(san);
+   510	      }
+   511	
+   512	      // Step 4 — cap on accepted nodes.
+   513	      if (sanitized.length > NODES_CAP) {
+   514	        return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.GRAPH_CAP_EXCEEDED], { detail: 'sanitized node count ' + sanitized.length + ' exceeds NODES_CAP=' + NODES_CAP });
+   515	      }
+   516	
+   517	      // Step 5 — RN-03 closure: sort sanitized by full canonical content BEFORE any dedup so caller
+   518	      // permutation cannot influence which duplicate wins. Tie-breaker on a deterministic field.
+   519	      sanitized.sort(function (a, b) { return _strcmp(_fullCanonicalString(a), _fullCanonicalString(b)); });
+   520	
+   521	      // Step 6 — duplicate-ID dedup (Object.create(null) map — RN-08 closure). First by sort order
+   522	      // (deterministic) is kept; subsequent rejected.
+   523	      var byId = Object.create(null);
+   524	      var keptById = [];
+   525	      var rejectedDuplicateIds = [];
+   526	      for (var d = 0; d < sanitized.length; d++) {
+   527	        var n = sanitized[d];
+   528	        if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(byId, n.nodeId)) {
+   529	          rejectedDuplicateIds.push(n.nodeId);
+   530	          tally(CODES.EVIDENCE_DUPLICATE_ID);
+   531	          rejectedCount++;
+   532	          continue;
+   533	        }
+   534	        byId[n.nodeId] = n;
+   535	        keptById.push(n);
+   536	      }
+   537	      rejectedDuplicateIds.sort(_strcmp);
+   538	
+   539	      // Step 7 — semantic CANONICAL STRING dedup (RN-04 closure). The map KEY is the full canonical
+   540	      // string itself, not a hash. Collisions are impossible by definition.
+   541	      var semSeen = Object.create(null);
+   542	      var keptAfterSemantic = [];
+   543	      var rejectedSemanticDuplicates = [];
+   544	      for (var s = 0; s < keptById.length; s++) {
+   545	        var nn = keptById[s];
+   546	        var canon = _semanticCanonicalString(nn);
+   547	        if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(semSeen, canon)) {
+   548	          rejectedSemanticDuplicates.push({ fingerprint: _hash64('sem|' + canon), keptNodeId: semSeen[canon], droppedNodeId: nn.nodeId });
+   549	          tally(CODES.EVIDENCE_GRAPH_DUPLICATED_SOURCE_DOUBLECOUNT);
+   550	          rejectedCount++;
+   551	          delete byId[nn.nodeId];
+   552	          continue;
+   553	        }
+   554	        semSeen[canon] = nn.nodeId;
+   555	        keptAfterSemantic.push(nn);
+   556	      }
+   557	      rejectedSemanticDuplicates.sort(function (a, b) { return _strcmp(a.droppedNodeId, b.droppedNodeId); });
+   558	
+   559	      // Step 8 — orphan edge detection. Any supportingEdges / contradictingEdges target nodeId that
+   560	      // is not in the surviving graph triggers EVIDENCE_GRAPH_ORPHAN.
+   561	      var orphanDetected = false;
+   562	      var orphanDetail = null;
+   563	      for (var e = 0; e < keptAfterSemantic.length; e++) {
+   564	        var nx = keptAfterSemantic[e];
+   565	        var allEdges = nx.supportingEdges.concat(nx.contradictingEdges);
+   566	        for (var ek = 0; ek < allEdges.length; ek++) {
+   567	          var tgt = allEdges[ek];
+   568	          if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(byId, tgt)) {
+   569	            orphanDetected = true;
+   570	            orphanDetail = 'node ' + nx.nodeId + ' references unknown nodeId ' + tgt;
+   571	            break;
+   572	          }
+   573	        }
+   574	        if (orphanDetected) break;
+   575	      }
+   576	      if (orphanDetected) {
+   577	        return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_GRAPH_ORPHAN], { detail: orphanDetail });
+   578	      }
+   579	
+   580	      // Step 9 — build edges + causal adjacency (RN-08: proto-null map).
+   581	      var edges = [];
+   582	      var adjacencyCausal = Object.create(null);
+   583	      function addEdge(from, to, kind) {
+   584	        if (EDGE_KIND_ALLOWED.indexOf(kind) === -1) return false;
+   585	        edges.push({ from: from, to: to, kind: kind });
+   586	        if (EDGE_KIND_CAUSAL[kind]) {
+   587	          if (!adjacencyCausal[from]) adjacencyCausal[from] = [];
+   588	          adjacencyCausal[from].push(to);
+   589	        }
+   590	        return true;
+   591	      }
+   592	      for (var f = 0; f < keptAfterSemantic.length; f++) {
+   593	        var nf = keptAfterSemantic[f];
+   594	        for (var sf = 0; sf < nf.supportingEdges.length; sf++) {
+   595	          if (nf.supportingEdges[sf] === nf.nodeId) {
+   596	            return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_GRAPH_SELF_REFERENCE], { detail: nf.nodeId + ' supportingEdges self-reference' });
+   597	          }
+   598	          addEdge(nf.nodeId, nf.supportingEdges[sf], 'supports');
+   599	        }
+   600	        for (var cf = 0; cf < nf.contradictingEdges.length; cf++) {
+   601	          if (nf.contradictingEdges[cf] === nf.nodeId) {
+   602	            return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_GRAPH_SELF_REFERENCE], { detail: nf.nodeId + ' contradictingEdges self-reference' });
+   603	          }
+   604	          addEdge(nf.nodeId, nf.contradictingEdges[cf], 'contradicts');
+   605	        }
+   606	      }
+   607	
+   608	      // Step 10 — correlation groups. Same canonical (caseId|sessionId|lapId|sourceId) → one group.
+   609	      var groupBucket = Object.create(null);
+   610	      for (var g = 0; g < keptAfterSemantic.length; g++) {
+   611	        var ng = keptAfterSemantic[g];
+   612	        var gk = _correlationGroupCanonical(ng);
+   613	        if (!groupBucket[gk]) groupBucket[gk] = [];
+   614	        groupBucket[gk].push(ng.nodeId);
+   615	      }
+   616	      var groupKeysSorted = Object.keys(groupBucket).sort(_strcmp);
+   617	      var correlationGroups = [];
+   618	      for (var gi = 0; gi < groupKeysSorted.length; gi++) {
+   619	        var gk2 = groupKeysSorted[gi];
+   620	        var members = groupBucket[gk2].slice().sort(_strcmp);
+   620	        var members = groupBucket[gk2].slice().sort(_strcmp);
+   621	        var iw = members.length > 0 ? 1 / members.length : 0;
+   622	        correlationGroups.push({
+   623	          correlationGroupId: _correlationGroupId(gk2),
+   624	          memberNodeIds: members,
+   625	          independenceWeight: iw,
+   626	        });
+   627	        if (members.length >= 2) {
+   628	          for (var mi = 0; mi < members.length; mi++) {
+   629	            for (var mj = mi + 1; mj < members.length; mj++) {
+   630	              var from = members[mi] < members[mj] ? members[mi] : members[mj];
+   631	              var to = members[mi] < members[mj] ? members[mj] : members[mi];
+   632	              addEdge(from, to, 'correlated_with');
+   633	            }
+   634	          }
+   635	        }
+   636	      }
+   637	      if (correlationGroups.length > CORRELATION_GROUPS_CAP) {
+   638	        return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.GRAPH_CAP_EXCEEDED], { detail: 'correlation groups ' + correlationGroups.length + ' exceeds CORRELATION_GROUPS_CAP=' + CORRELATION_GROUPS_CAP });
+   639	      }
+   640	
+   641	      // Step 11 — edge cap.
+   642	      if (edges.length > EDGES_CAP_TOTAL) {
+   643	        return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.GRAPH_CAP_EXCEEDED], { detail: 'edges ' + edges.length + ' exceeds EDGES_CAP_TOTAL=' + EDGES_CAP_TOTAL });
+   644	      }
+   645	
+   646	      // Step 12 — cycle detection over CAUSAL edges only.
+   647	      var sortedNodeIds = keptAfterSemantic.map(function (x) { return x.nodeId; }).slice().sort(_strcmp);
+   648	      var cycleHit = _findCycle(sortedNodeIds, adjacencyCausal);
+   649	      if (cycleHit !== null) {
+   650	        return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_GRAPH_CYCLE], { detail: 'cycle involves nodeId ' + cycleHit });
+   651	      }
+   652	
+   653	      // Step 13 — topological order over the acyclic causal subgraph.
+   654	      var topo = _topologicalOrder(sortedNodeIds, adjacencyCausal);
+   655	
+   656	      // Step 14 — sort nodes deterministically; sort edges by (from, to, kind).
+   657	      var nodesOut = keptAfterSemantic.slice().sort(function (a, b) { return _strcmp(a.nodeId, b.nodeId); });
+   658	      var edgesOut = edges.slice().sort(function (a, b) {
+   659	        var c = _strcmp(a.from, b.from); if (c !== 0) return c;
+   660	        c = _strcmp(a.to, b.to); if (c !== 0) return c;
+   661	        return _strcmp(a.kind, b.kind);
+   662	      });
+   663	      for (var ni = 0; ni < nodesOut.length; ni++) Object.freeze(nodesOut[ni]);
+   664	      for (var ei = 0; ei < edgesOut.length; ei++) Object.freeze(edgesOut[ei]);
+   665	
+   666	      // Step 15 — limitations / cannotConclude derivation.
+   667	      var limitations = [];
+   668	      var cannotConclude = [];
+   669	      var limSeen = Object.create(null);
+   670	      for (var li = 0; li < nodesOut.length; li++) {
+   671	        var lims = nodesOut[li].limitations;
+   672	        for (var lj = 0; lj < lims.length; lj++) {
+   673	          if (!limSeen[lims[lj]]) { limSeen[lims[lj]] = true; limitations.push(lims[lj]); }
+   674	        }
+   675	      }
+   676	      if (nodesOut.length === 0) {
+   677	        if (cannotConclude.indexOf(CODES.INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE) === -1) cannotConclude.push(CODES.INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE);
+   678	      }
+   679	      if (rejectedCount > 0) {
+   680	        if (cannotConclude.indexOf(CODES.CANNOT_CONCLUDE) === -1) cannotConclude.push(CODES.CANNOT_CONCLUDE);
+   681	      }
+   682	      limitations.sort(_strcmp);
+   683	      cannotConclude.sort(_strcmp);
+   684	      if (limitations.length > LIMITATIONS_CAP) limitations = limitations.slice(0, LIMITATIONS_CAP);
+   685	      if (cannotConclude.length > CANNOT_CONCLUDE_CAP) cannotConclude = cannotConclude.slice(0, CANNOT_CONCLUDE_CAP);
+   686	
+   687	      // Step 16 — derive graphId (content-only; clock-independent).
+   688	      var idPayload = {
+   689	        caseAssociation: caseAssociation,
+   690	        nodes: nodesOut.map(function (n) {
+   691	          return {
+   692	            nodeId: n.nodeId,
+   693	            category: n.category,
+   694	            credibility: n.credibility,
+   695	            provenance: n.provenance,
+   696	            availability: n.availability,
+   697	            identity: {
+   698	              caseId: n.identity.caseId,
+   699	              sessionId: n.identity.sessionId,
+   700	              lapId: n.identity.lapId,
+   701	              sourceId: n.identity.sourceId,
+   702	              sourceVersion: n.identity.sourceVersion,
+   703	            },
+   704	            observation: {
+   705	              kind: n.observation.kind,
+   706	              channel: n.observation.channel,
+   707	              i18nKey: n.observation.i18nKey,
+   708	              params: n.observation.params,
+   709	            },
+   710	            limitations: n.limitations,
+   711	            supportingEdges: n.supportingEdges,
+   712	            contradictingEdges: n.contradictingEdges,
+   713	          };
+   714	        }),
+   715	        edges: edgesOut,
+   716	        correlationGroups: correlationGroups.map(function (g) { return { correlationGroupId: g.correlationGroupId, memberNodeIds: g.memberNodeIds, independenceWeight: g.independenceWeight }; }),
+   717	      };
+   718	      var graphId = 'graph_' + _hash64('graphid|v' + GRAPH_SCHEMA_VERSION + '|' + _stableStringify(idPayload));
+   719	
+   720	      // Step 17 — RN-09 closure: post-sanitization JSON byte cap. Safe to JSON.stringify because every
+   721	      // sanitized value is a plain frozen primitive (no accessors, no Proxies, no class instances).
+   722	      var envBytes;
+   723	      try {
+   724	        var s = JSON.stringify(idPayload);
+   725	        envBytes = (typeof TextEncoder !== 'undefined') ? new TextEncoder().encode(s).length : Buffer.byteLength(s, 'utf8');
+   726	      } catch (e) { envBytes = Infinity; }
+   727	      if (envBytes > ENVELOPE_BYTE_CAP) {
+   728	        return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.BYTE_CAP_EXCEEDED], { detail: 'post-sanitization JSON size ' + envBytes + ' exceeds ENVELOPE_BYTE_CAP=' + ENVELOPE_BYTE_CAP });
+   729	      }
+   730	
+   731	      // Step 18 — final materialisation
+   732	      var graph = _materializeGraph({
+   733	        graphId: graphId,
+   734	        caseAssociation: caseAssociation,
+   735	        nodes: nodesOut,
+   736	        edges: edgesOut,
+   737	        topologicalOrder: topo,
+   738	        dedup: {
+   739	          rejectedDuplicateIds: rejectedDuplicateIds,
+   740	          rejectedSemanticDuplicates: rejectedSemanticDuplicates,
+   741	          rejectedSourceReplays: [],
+   742	        },
+   743	        correlationGroups: correlationGroups,
+   744	        limitations: limitations,
+   745	        cannotConclude: cannotConclude,
+   746	        provenance: {
+   747	          inputCount: inputCount,
+   748	          sanitizedCount: nodesOut.length,
+   749	          rejectedCount: rejectedCount,
+   750	          rejectedReasonsSummary: rejectedReasons,
+   751	        },
+   752	        createdAt: createdAt,
+   753	        generationToken: generationToken,
+   754	        contextVersion: contextVersion,
+   755	      });
+   756	
+   757	      return Object.freeze({ valid: true, graph: graph });
+   758	    } catch (e) {
+   759	      return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.INTERNAL_CONTRACT_VIOLATION], { detail: 'buildEvidenceGraph threw on hostile input' });
+   760	    }
+   761	  }
+   762	
+   763	  // Public API surface — RN-07 closure: internal helpers (_stableStringify, _semanticCanonicalString,
+   764	  // _correlationGroupCanonical) are NOT exported. The only public entry is buildEvidenceGraph plus
+   765	  // documented constants. Tests must drive the contract via buildEvidenceGraph only.
+     1	/**
+     2	 * tests/r3.0d-evidence-graph.test.js — R3.0D D2 · Evidence Graph adversarial suite.
+     3	 *
+     4	 * Verifies the D2 builder:
+     5	 *   • valid graph (golden path)
+     6	 *   • empty graph policy (returns valid with INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE cannotConclude)
+     7	 *   • duplicate nodeId rejection (first kept, accounting recorded)
+     8	 *   • semantic-fingerprint dedup (same source content under different nodeId)
+     9	 *   • correlation grouping (same source-identity grouped, independenceWeight = 1/N)
+    10	 *   • directed cycle / multi-node cycle → fail-closed EVIDENCE_GRAPH_CYCLE
+    11	 *   • self-edge (post-rename smuggle attempt) → fail-closed EVIDENCE_GRAPH_SELF_REFERENCE
+    12	 *   • orphan target edge → fail-closed EVIDENCE_GRAPH_ORPHAN
+    13	 *   • stale freshness still passes D1 (shape only); association mismatch (wrong case/session/lap)
+    14	 *   • imported_summary elevation guard → EVIDENCE_IMPORTED_SUMMARY_ELEVATION_FORBIDDEN
+    15	 *   • forged provenance / forged credibility (D1 rejects synthetic / heuristic without limitation)
+    16	 *   • prototype-pollution / Proxy ownKeys / throwing getter / accessor descriptor / Symbol key /
+    17	 *     non-enumerable key / class instance / nested class / Array subclass / mutated array prototype /
+    18	 *     cyclic caller input / NaN / Infinity / oversized string / oversized array / oversized graph
+    19	 *   • deterministic node + edge + topological order + serialization
+    20	 *   • deep freeze (caller cannot mutate returned graph)
+    21	 *   • caller mutates input array after build → graph unchanged (no shared reference)
+    22	 *   • raw telemetry tunnel attempt (params contains oversized string) → BYTE_CAP_EXCEEDED via D1
+    23	 *   • private path / filename-shaped nodeId → EVIDENCE_NODE_ID_FORBIDDEN via D1
+    24	 *   • future schema rejection (schemaVersion > SUPPORTED_SCHEMA_VERSION) via D1
+    25	 *   • injectable clock; missing clock → createdAt null; throwing clock → null fail-closed
+    26	 *
+    27	 * Node CLI: `node tests/r3.0d-evidence-graph.test.js`, exit 1 on any failure.
+    28	 */

+    44	  }
